# Parkview -- what each voice can say, for the people in the scenes. See Core/Voices.cs.
# Made from menyooStuff\PedSpeechList.txt: every voice, with only the lines somebody stood about
# talking says -- chat, greetings, answers, rants, a word about your car or your gun, the gang's
# threats, and phone calls. Nothing from a fight, a chase, a car or a death.
# A ped's voice is the one named after his model (picked by his mark when there are several),
# A model under [models] is given that voice instead -- one with none of its own, or one whose
# own has too little to say. Anybody else: the default for a man or a woman.

[models]
a_m_o_ktown_01 = A_M_M_KTOWN_01_KOREAN_FULL_01

s_m_y_prismuscl_01 = PRISONER
mp_m_weapexp_01 = A_M_M_SKATER_01_WHITE_FULL_01
male = A_M_Y_SOUCENT_02_BLACK_FULL_01
female = A_F_Y_SOUCENT_01_BLACK_FULL_01
